# Tech Lead Implementation Reports Log

This directory centralizes the history and traceability of all User Story implementations, technical decisions, and architectural evolutions in **ORCIA**.

---

## 📋 Mandatory Implementation Standard

For every User Story (`Story N.M`) **and** for every ad-hoc adjustment, bugfix, or evolution request in ORCIA, a permanent Tech Lead report must be authored in this directory **before making any code modification** following the naming convention:
- `{YYYY-MM-DD}-{short-slug}-tech-lead-report.md` (prefixed with today's date followed by a short kebab-case description of the changes for any out-of-story adjustment/bugfix);
- `{story-key}-tech-lead-report.md` (for a sprint User Story, based on the official template [`../TECH-LEAD-REPORT-TEMPLATE.md`](../TECH-LEAD-REPORT-TEMPLATE.md)).

Every document **must contain the following 3 sections**:

1. **Needs Analysis & Tech Lead Impact Study (Pre-Implementation)**:
   - Business / technical context and problem statement.
   - GitNexus impact analysis (`gitnexus_query`, `gitnexus_context`, `gitnexus_impact`).
   - Exploration & key metrics (database schema, file sizes, edge cases).
   - Factorization (`DRY`) opportunities & created technical debt tickets (`sprint-status.yaml` / `epics.md`).
2. **Implementation Plan**:
   - Data schema & Alembic migrations (`infra/migrations/versions/`).
   - Hexagonal core, algorithms & workers (`packages/orcia-core/`, `apps/worker/`, `infra/scripts/maintenance/`).
   - FastAPI backend layer & OpenAPI contracts (`apps/api/`, `packages/api-client/`).
   - Design System (`packages/ui` / `@orcia/ui`), `i18n` (`fr`/`en`) & Vite + TanStack Router Direction C SPA (`apps/web/`).
3. **Summary of Changes & Results (Post-Implementation)**:
   - Summary table of created and modified files.
   - Quantitative metrics, test results & branch coverage ($\ge 90\%$).
   - Validation across all 5 test layers (`AD-17`), the 5 vertical wiring guardrails, and `gitnexus_detect_changes()` output.
